Result: MCMC算法能够在广泛的问题规模范围内可靠地达到DQI预期的近似比;在OPI问题中,在声称DQI具有超多项式优势的区域,观察到MCMC的经验运行时间近似按1.1^n的速度增长,表现出指数增长但基数相对较小;在max-XORSAT问题上扩展到超过1000个有效量子比特,在OPI问题上扩展到超过150个有效量子比特。

Conclusion: 研究结果并未反驳现有的量子优势声明,但提供了新的实证证据表明经典采样算法可以紧密匹配DQI的优化性能,为DQI的实际优势提供了更加细致的视角。这表明在评估量子算法的实际优势时,需要更全面地考虑经典算法的性能表现。

Abstract: Optimization problems are among the leading candidates for industrially relevant quantum advantage. Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) has been proposed to tackle approximate optimization, establishing a connection to classical decoding problems. While previous work has primarily focused on the theoretical complexity of DQI, comparatively little is known about its empirical performance relative to classical algorithms. In this work, we shed further light on the complexity of DQI and investigate numerically whether classical sampling methods can emulate the optimization capabilities of DQI. We first present a simplified analytical characterization of DQI that connects its expected performance to binomial statistics, and we identify concrete obstacles in further studying the complexity of DQI. Exploiting the fact that DQI output probabilities are efficiently computable, we apply Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) techniques, particularly block-Gibbs sampling, to sample from the induced distribution. We study the runtime scaling of these methods for two optimization problems called max-XORSAT, where we reach beyond $1000$ effective qubits; and OPI, where we reach beyond $150$ effective qubits. Our results show that MCMC algorithms can reliably attain the approximation ratios expected from DQI across a broad range of problem sizes. In OPI, in the regime where a super-polynomial advantage is claimed for DQI, we observe an empirical runtime for MCMC that scales approximately as $1.1^{n}$, indicating exponential growth with a comparatively small base. Our findings do not refute existing quantum advantage claims but provide new empirical evidence that classical sampling algorithms can closely match DQI's optimization performance, offering a more nuanced perspective on the practical advantage of DQI.

</details>


### [88] [Efficient atom rearrangements for quantum error correction primitives with a single AOD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28149)
*Tom Hartweg,Asier Piñeiro Orioli,Hugo Perrin,Samuel Crew*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在中性原子量子计算机中优化原子阵列几何重排的新方法,通过剪切、旋转和反射等基本操作原语,利用声光偏转器(AOD)实现了对数级别的操作复杂度缩放,显著提升了量子逻辑门操作的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算机依赖原子传输实现任意连接性,许多逻辑操作可简化为原子的几何重排。为了提高逻辑吞吐量,必须最小化这些原子移动的持续时间。现有的逐原子重排方法在操作复杂度和时间成本上都随阵列尺寸呈多项式增长,限制了量子计算的效率。因此需要开发更高效的原子阵列操作方法。

Method: 引入了在静态晶格中使用单对动态交叉声光偏转器(AOD)扫描来实现2D原子阵列的剪切、旋转和反射等新操作原语。采用(负)二进制分解和几何分解技术,使AOD扫描次数相对于阵列线性尺寸呈对数级缩放。具体以Paeth分解为例,实现旋转表面码中横向Hadamard门所需的90度旋转操作。

Result: 对于距离参数为d的旋转表面码,实现90度旋转仅需$3\lfloor\log_2(d-1)\rfloor + 4$次AOD扫描和$O(d^{1/3})$恒定加加速度时间,相比传统的逐原子重排方法需要$O(d^2)$次扫描和$O(d^{7/3})$时间,实现了指数级的性能提升。该方法显著减少了操作步骤和执行时间。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于几何分解的新型原子阵列操作原语,成功将中性原子量子计算机中原子重排的复杂度从多项式级降低到对数级,大幅提高了量子逻辑门操作的效率和吞吐量。这为中性原子量子计算平台的可扩展性和实用化提供了重要的技术支撑,特别是在表面码等量子纠错方案的实现中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Neutral-atom quantum computers offer arbitrary connectivity enabled by atom transport. Some logical operations can then be simplified or reduced entirely to geometric rearrangements of the atoms. Minimizing the duration of these movements is therefore essential for high logical throughput. We introduce new primitives to shear, rotate and reflect 2D arrays of atoms in a static lattice using sweeps of a single dynamic crossed acousto-optic deflector (AOD) pair. Using (nega-)binary and geometric decompositions, we achieve an AOD stroke count scaling logarithmically in the linear size of the array. In one example, we use the Paeth decomposition to implement a $90^{\circ}$ rotation for a transversal Hadamard gate in a rotated surface code of distance $d$ in $3\lfloor\log_2(d-1)\rfloor + 4$ AOD strokes and $O(d^{1/3})$ constant-jerk time, against $O(d^2)$ strokes and $O(d^{7/3})$ time for atom-by-atom rearrangement.

</details>


### [89] [Entanglement Swapping with Integrated Narrowband Photon Sources for Quantum Repeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28184)
*Mingsong Wu,Leili Esmaeilifar,Rui Ning Wang,Tobias Kippenberg,Rob Thew,Tiff Brydges*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了使用与原子量子存储器兼容的集成窄带光子源实现纠缠交换,获得了0.99的高HOM可见度和0.88的交换态可见度,并通过全光纤商用组件实现了跨越1.6 THz的相位和频率稳定,为现场部署的量子中继器提供了重要的技术解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一代量子中继器需要原子量子存储器与光子源接口,集成光子学虽然适合光纤部署,但难以产生与主流量子存储平台兼容的窄带光子。此外,使用窄带光子实现纠缠交换在系统级稳定性方面面临重大技术挑战。本研究旨在解决这些基础性和技术性难题,推动现场部署量子中继器的实用化进程。

Method: 使用最先进的集成光子源产生带宽与多种原子量子存储平台兼容的窄带光子对;采用独立泵浦激光器为每个光子对源供能,模拟不同中继节点或平台之间的纠缠交换;使用全光纤商用组件实现跨越1.6 THz频率范围的相位和频率稳定;通过Hong-Ou-Mandel(HOM)干涉测量光子不可区分性和纯度,并测量交换后的纠缠态可见度以验证纠缠质量。

Result: 实现了扣除背景后(净值)HOM可见度为0.99±0.01,表明光子具有高度不可区分性和纯度;获得了净交换态可见度为0.88±0.06,该值足以违反贝尔不等式,证明最终纠缠态质量满足量子信息处理要求;成功在频率差异极大的独立光源之间实现了系统级稳定,验证了技术方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了现场部署量子中继器的关键挑战,包括开发与原子量子存储器兼容的集成窄带光子源,以及实现独立量子中继节点之间的系统级稳定。高质量的纠缠交换结果和基于商用光纤组件的稳定方案,为构建实用化、可部署的量子中继网络奠定了重要技术基础,推动了量子通信从实验室向实际应用的转化。

Abstract: Promising implementations of first generation quantum repeaters are predicted to require atomic-based quantum memory systems interfaced with photonic sources. Integrated photonics provides a promising solution for fibre-based, field-deployed operation of quantum repeaters, however many leading quantum memory platforms require narrow-bandwidth photons that are challenging to generate with integrated photonics. Narrowband photons also present significant technical challenges when implementing entanglement-swapping, particularly with regards to systems-level stabilisation. This work addresses some of these fundamental and technical challenges, by demonstrating entanglement-swapping using state-of-the-art integrated photon sources with bandwidths compatible with multiple atomic-based quantum memory platforms. We obtained a background-subtracted (net) HOM visibility of 0.99$\,\pm\,$0.01, showing high photon indistinguishability and purity, with a net swapped state visibility of $\mathcal{V}$=0.88$\,\pm\,$0.06 demonstrating that the final entanglement would be sufficient to violate a Bell inequality. The experiment used independent pump lasers for each photon pair source, with highly different frequencies to mimic entanglement swapping between different repeater nodes or platforms. Phase and frequency stabilisation spanning 1.6 THz was achieved using all-fibre, commercially-available components. These results address important challenges in implementing field-deployed quantum repeaters, from the integrated photonic solutions for narrowband photon pairs, to systems-level stabilisation between independent quantum repeater nodes.

</details>


### [90] [Purifications for Convex Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28202)
*Felix Campidell,Tim Netzer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从有限维正常凸锥的几何角度研究量子理论中的纯化原理,证明了不可分解锥和包含最大纠缠态的中间张量锥的纯化存在性定理,并给出了局部自同构意义下唯一性的判别准则,同时探讨了边界点的纯化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯化原理在量子理论和广义概率理论中具有重要地位,但其几何本质尚未被充分理解。本文旨在仅利用有限维正常凸锥的几何结构来研究纯化现象,从而揭示纯化原理的数学基础,并将其推广到更一般的凸锥框架中。

Method: 采用凸几何和锥理论的方法:1)对不可分解锥和包含最大纠缠态的中间张量锥建立纯化存在性定理;2)特别研究不可分解齐次锥的内点,证明其纯化的存在性;3)建立局部自同构意义下纯化唯一性的判别准则;4)分析边界情形,利用面的单纯性刻画可纯化点;5)通过半正定锥、Lorentz锥、k-正映射、PPT张量和多面体锥等具体例子验证理论结果。

Result: 证明了不可分解齐次锥的每个内点都承认纯化(适用于Lorentz锥等);建立了纯化在局部自同构意义下唯一性的判别准则;对于边界情况,证明了当C的每个正常面都是单纯形时,仅纯点可承认纯化,并展示了存在非单纯面时该结论不成立;通过多个具体例子(正半定锥、Lorentz锥、k-正映射、PPT张量、多面体锥)验证了存在性和非唯一性现象。

Conclusion: 本文从纯几何角度阐明了量子纯化原理的数学本质,证明了在正常凸锥框架下纯化的存在性和唯一性条件。研究表明纯化现象不依赖于量子力学的特殊结构,而是凸锥几何的内在性质。不可分解齐次锥的内点总是可纯化的,边界点的可纯化性则与面的几何结构密切相关。这些结果为广义概率理论提供了坚实的几何基础,并揭示了不同物理系统中纯化行为的统一数学结构。

Abstract: Motivated by the importance of the purification principle in quantum theory and generalized probabilistic theories, we study purifications using only the geometry of a finite-dimensional proper convex cone. We prove an existence theorem for indecomposable cones and intermediate tensor cones containing the maximally entangled state; in particular, every interior point of an indecomposable homogeneous cone admits a purification. This applies to Lorentz cones, for example. We also give a criterion for uniqueness up to local automorphisms. On the boundary, we show that if every proper face of $C$ is simplicial, then only pure points can admit purifications, and we demonstrate that this conclusion fails in the presence of non-simplicial faces. Examples involving positive semidefinite cones, Lorentz cones, $k$-positive maps, PPT tensors, and polyhedral cones illustrate both existence and non-uniqueness phenomena.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum Arago-Fresnel interference of displaced spin states of photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28231)

TL;DR: 本文基于现代非经典态描述重新研究了由光子螺旋度产生的宏观Arago-Fresnel干涉现象,通过计算相干态与位移单光子自旋态组合光束的Stokes参数,揭示了自旋取向及其相对不对称性对干涉条纹的影响,并建立了一种纯光学方法来确定未知入射光子的自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arago和Fresnel四定律历史性地通过共面性判据建立了光束干涉能力与偏振的关系。然而在现代量子光学框架下,需要重新审视光子螺旋度(helicity)在宏观Arago-Fresnel干涉中的作用机制,探索非经典态(如单光子自旋态)与经典相干态组合时的干涉特性,以深化对光子偏振本质的量子理解并开发新的量子测量手段。

Method: 采用量子光学理论框架,计算由两种不同态组合而成的偏振光束的Stokes参数。具体包括:(1)一束常规相干态(从真空态位移得到);(2)一束位移单光子自旋态(从左旋或右旋单光子态位移得到)。通过分析自旋取向以及相对于位移相干态偏振方向的相对不对称性,研究这些参数对Stokes参数的依赖关系,进而揭示不同的干涉条纹模式。利用量子干涉测量技术建立光子自旋的光学判定方法。

Result: 研究结果显示,光子的自旋取向(左旋或右旋)及其相对于位移相干态偏振取向的不对称性,在Stokes参数中产生了可区分的参数依赖特征,从而形成不同的干涉条纹图样。这些独特的参数依赖关系和干涉图案可作为光子自旋态的特征指纹。基于此发现,成功建立了一种纯光学量子干涉测量方法,能够通过观测干涉条纹特性来确定未知入射光子的自旋状态。

Conclusion: 本研究从量子态描述角度重新诠释了经典的Arago-Fresnel干涉定律,证明了光子螺旋度在宏观干涉现象中的可观测效应。通过相干态与位移单光子自旋态的组合干涉分析,建立了自旋取向、相对不对称性与干涉条纹之间的明确对应关系,为光的偏振概念提供了更深层的量子力学理解。同时,该量子干涉测量技术为光子自旋的纯光学表征提供了新方法,具有潜在的量子信息处理和精密测量应用价值。

Abstract: The four laws by Arago and Fresnel distinguish the coplanarity of two light beams to determine their capacity of interference, laying the historic milestone for conceptualizing the polarization of light. Equipped with modern descriptions of non-classical states, we re-investigate the macroscopic Arago-Fresnel interference producible by photon helicities. To this end, we compute the Stokes parameter of a polarized beam combined from a regular coherent state (displaced from the vacuum) and a displaced single-photon spin state (displaced from either a left- or right-spin state of photon). The spin orientation, together with its relative asymmetry with respect to the polarizing orientation of the displacing coherent state, produces distinguishing parameter dependences and thus distict interference fringes. Conversely, this quantum interferometry establishes a purely optical method to determine the spin of an unknown incident photon.

</details>


### [92] [Optimal T Counts under Sparsity: from QROM to State Preparation and Block Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28260)
*Tongyang Li,Fengning Ou,Xinzhao Wang,Penghui Yao,Pei Yuan,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏量子只读存储器(sparse QROM)的T门计数复杂度,证明了渐近最优的$Θ(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$界,其中$s$为支撑集大小,$m$为消息长度,$n$为地址位数,并将结果应用于稀疏态制备和稀疏矩阵块编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法通常需要相干访问经典数据,这常通过量子只读存储器(QROM)建模。然而,在许多实际应用中,数据是稀疏的,即$2^n$个地址中只有$s$个存储非零数据。现有QROM的T门计数复杂度研究未充分考虑稀疏性带来的优化空间。因此,本文旨在研究稀疏QROM的T门计数,以利用数据稀疏性降低量子电路的资源开销,这对于容错量子计算中T门成本高昂的问题具有重要意义。

Method: 上界证明:采用多层哈希方案(multilevel hashing scheme)构造稀疏QROM电路,实现$O(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$的T门计数。该方法利用数据的稀疏结构,通过分层哈希减少访问非零元素所需的量子资源。

下界证明:将稀疏QROM问题归约到态制备问题,并使用自适应Clifford+T电路的计数论证(counting arguments)建立$Ω(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$的下界。该下界即使在允许中间测量和经典控制操作的情况下也成立,表明这是本质性的复杂度限制。

应用扩展:将核心结果推广到$s$-稀疏态制备和$s$-稀疏矩阵块编码问题,分别导出相应的匹配上下界。

Result: 1. 稀疏QROM的T门计数渐近最优界为$Θ(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$,其中对支撑集大小$s$和消息长度$m$均呈平方根依赖关系。

2. $s$-稀疏态制备的T门计数匹配界为$Θ(\sqrt{sn} + \sqrt{s\log(1/\varepsilon)} + \log(1/\varepsilon))$,其中$\varepsilon$为态制备精度。

3. $s$-稀疏矩阵块编码的T门计数匹配界为$Θ(\sqrt{2^n sn} + \sqrt{2^n s\log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}})} + \log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}))$,其中$\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}$为块编码精度。

4. 所有下界在允许中间测量和经典控制的更强计算模型下仍然成立,说明结果具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了稀疏量子只读存储器T门计数复杂度的渐近紧致界$Θ(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$,揭示了数据稀疏性可以带来平方根级别的资源节省。通过多层哈希上界构造和基于态制备归约的下界证明,证明了该界是最优的。研究结果对稀疏态制备和稀疏矩阵块编码等重要量子算法原语具有直接应用价值,为容错量子计算中利用数据结构特性优化资源开销提供了理论基础,对降低实用量子算法的实现成本具有重要意义。

Abstract: Many quantum algorithms require coherent access to classical data, often modeled by quantum read-only memory (QROM). We initiate the study of the $T$ count of sparse QROM, in which only $s$ of the $2^n$ addresses store nonzero data. We prove asymptotically optimal $T$-count bounds $Θ(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$ with square-root dependence on the support size $s$ and message length $m$. Our upper bounds use a multilevel hashing scheme, while our lower bounds reduce sparse QROM to state preparation and use counting arguments for adaptive Clifford+$T$ circuits. The lower bounds thus hold even when mid-circuit measurements and classically controlled operations are allowed. As applications, we obtain matching $T$-count bounds $Θ(\sqrt{sn} + \sqrt{s\log(1/\varepsilon)} +
  \log(1/\varepsilon))$ for $s$-sparse state preparation and $Θ( \sqrt{2^n sn}
  +
  \sqrt{2^n s\log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}})}
  +
  \log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}))$ for block encoding of $s$-sparse matrices, where $\varepsilon$ and $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}$ are the precision of state preparation and block encoding, respectively.

</details>


### [93] [No-Go Theorems for Quantum Transport Metrics from Fixed Cost Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28358)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Tianshi Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于耦合的量子最优传输在固定成本算子下无法在完整量子态空间上构成度量,通过统一的否定性定理否定了Friedland等人关于SWAP投影算子的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Friedland等人猜想与SWAP投影算子相关的最优成本的平方根在任意维度下都是度量,且该性质对邻近的量子成本矩阵也成立。Miller通过构造显式的对角qutrit反例否定了这两个猜想。本文旨在建立更一般和系统的理论框架,全面分析固定成本耦合形式在量子态空间上能否构成度量。

Method: 本文采用理论证明方法,针对标准耦合和通道诱导耦合两种情况分别建立否定性定理。对于标准耦合,在Miller反例的基础上进行分析,证明在维度d≥3时,任何固定成本算子都无法使最优成本或其平方根成为度量,即使在对易态之间也会出现违反度量公理的情况,并且这种障碍在SWAP成本的稳定化条件下依然存在。对于通道诱导耦合,证明全局非负性和自成本为零的条件会迫使成本算子为零,从而在d≥2时无法实现点分离。

Result: 研究证明了统一的否定性定理:在维度d≥3的任意量子系统中,不存在固定的成本算子能使最优成本或其平方根成为度量,违反度量公理的情况已经在对易态中出现。该障碍在SWAP成本稳定化后仍然持续存在。对于通道诱导耦合(d≥2),全局非负性和自成本消失条件会导致成本算子必须为零,无法实现点分离性质。

Conclusion: 本文的否定性结果表明,固定成本耦合的量子最优传输形式化方法无法在完整的量子态空间上产生度量。这从根本上限制了基于耦合的量子最优传输理论作为量子态距离度量的应用前景,说明需要探索超越固定成本算子框架的新方法来构建量子态空间上的度量结构。

Abstract: Coupling-based quantum optimal transport generalizes classical optimal transport by representing transport plans as bipartite states with prescribed marginals and evaluating their cost as the expectation of a fixed Hermitian operator. Friedland et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 129, 110402 (2022)] conjectured that the square root of the optimal cost associated with the SWAP projector is a metric in every dimension and that this property persists for nearby quantum cost matrices. Miller [arXiv:2607.07764] disproved both conjectures by constructing explicit diagonal qutrit counterexamples. Building on his analysis, we prove a uniform no-go theorem for standard couplings. In every dimension $d\geq3$, no fixed cost operator makes either the optimal cost or its square root a metric, with violations occurring already among commuting states. The obstruction persists under stabilization of the SWAP cost. For channel-induced couplings, global nonnegativity and vanishing self-cost force the cost operator to be zero, precluding point separation when $d\geq2$. Taken together, these no-go results show that fixed-cost coupling formulations do not lead to metrics on the full quantum state space.

Beyond reproducing the data, the framework isolates which physical mechanism dominates in each operating regime, provides quantitative guidance for algorithm design and hardware improvements, and establishes a foundation for verifying analog processors in regimes beyond classical reach.

On one side, the ground state corresponds to a ferromagnetic phase, shows the presence of macroscopic cat states, and a small gap that closes in the thermodynamic limit. On the other phase, competing interactions avoid the establishment of a topological phase, though it conserves large incommensurate quantum correlations. We prove it is fundamentally distinct from a simple paramagnet, and we name it an incommensurate phase. This is a gapped phase, which gap does not close in the thermodynamic limit. While in the ferromagnetic phase there are two dominant Schmidt coefficients, in the incommensurate phase there are four. This corresponds to four distinct bipartite entanglement channels contributing substantially to the ground state. Finally, we discuss the utility of the macroscopic cat states for quantum metrology applications and the experimental feasibility of the system.

Abstract: Counterdiabatic (CD) driving accelerates adiabatic protocols by prescribing auxiliary control fields, but often fails to map them to physically available operations. We derive a general formalism for such a mapping. We formulate CD driving in a variational dressed frame, where an unconstrained auxiliary generator reshapes the effective adiabatic problem while simultaneously forcing the applied correction to stay restricted to the native laboratory controls. This yields a laboratory-frame commutator equation that can be solved without constructing instantaneous eigenstates or the adiabatic and dressed-frame unitaries. The additional dressed-frame freedom reveals solutions that are inaccessible in the conventional adiabatic frame. We illustrate this mechanism in three settings: suppression of spectator errors in a multi-qubit chain driven by a single quadrature, an analytical acceleration of adiabatic Bell-state preparation with a fixed entangling interaction, and implementation of a fast adiabatic holonomic gate in a degenerate tripod manifold with correction pulses confined to the native couplings. Our results provide a systematic nonperturbative framework for constructing implementable counterdiabatic protocols beyond the conventional adiabatic frame.

Abstract: Quantum oracle of Boolean functions is one of the central bridges between classical and quantum algorithms, but the study focusing at quantum circuit optimization of such oracle is yet closed. In this paper, we propose nearly optimal tradeoffs among circuit size, circuit depth and ancilla count, for quantum oracles of three kinds of Boolean functions:
  general total Boolean functions with output size $b$: with $1\le m\leΘ\left(\frac{2^n}{n}\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{b2^n}{\log(n+m)}\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{b2^n}{n+m}\right)$;
  partial Boolean functions of effective support size $d$ and output size $b$: with $Θ\left(\log d\right)\le m\le Θ\left(d\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(n\log d+bd\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{n\log n\log d}{n+m}+\log n+\frac{d(\log d+b\log m)}{m}\right)$;
  sparse Boolean functions of true input size $d$: with $Θ\left(\log n+\log d\right)\le m\leΘ\left(\frac{nd}{\log d}\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(n^2\log d+\frac{nd}{\log(\log d+m/n)}\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{n^2\log n\log d}{n+m}+\log n+\frac{nd}{m}\right)$.
  All the size and depth bounds are asymptotically optimal up to logarithmic factors in the corresponding ancilla count regions. We hope these results find applications in scenarios where classical procedures are needed to be embedded into quantum circuits, such as QROM implementation and quantum algorithm design.

Abstract: We extend the regime of convergence of Carleman-embedded quantum algorithms that solve the Vlasov-Poisson equations from kinetic plasma physics. The complexity of the quantum algorithm depends strongly on whether we seek time-averaged or -resolved outputs.

Result: 针对欧式看涨期权的数值实验表明,所提出的SFFT方法在保持定价精度的同时,相比传统基于FFT的定价方法显著降低了内存需求,并实现了亚指数级的计算扩展性。量子模拟和硬件执行结果显示,经典张量网络方法和基于QFT的量子方法都避免了传统傅里叶实现的指数级扩展问题,为大规模期权定价提供了互补的视角。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个连接经典傅里叶定价、张量网络算法和量子计算方法的统一框架,证明了张量化傅里叶方法可以为高维金融计算提供可扩展的替代方案。该方法成功克服了传统傅里叶定价的内存瓶颈,为金融衍生品定价的大规模计算开辟了新的技术路径,同时为经典和量子计算方法在金融领域的应用提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Fourier-based methods are among the most widely used techniques for pricing European options when the characteristic function of the underlying asset process is available. Their applicability to increasingly fine discretizations, however, is limited by the rapidly growing memory requirements of classical Fourier transforms, which become a computational bottleneck for large-scale pricing problems.
  In this work, we overcome this limitation by reformulating the Carr-Madan pricing framework using tensor networks. Specifically, we employ the Superfast Fourier Transform (SFFT), a compressed Tensor Train representation of the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT), and apply it directly to tensorized option pricing without ever explicitly constructing exponentially large vectors or Fourier operators. This formulation also enables a direct comparison between the classical tensor network algorithm and its quantum counterpart through QFT-based option pricing on quantum simulators and quantum hardware. Numerical experiments for European call options demonstrate that the proposed SFFT method maintains pricing accuracy while substantially reducing memory requirements and achieving subexponential computational scaling compared with conventional FFT-based pricing. The accompanying quantum simulations and hardware executions enable a direct comparison between the classical tensor network formulation and its QFT-based quantum counterpart, showing that both approaches avoid the exponential scaling of conventional Fourier implementations and provide complementary perspectives on large-scale option pricing.
  Together, these results establish a unified framework connecting classical Fourier pricing, tensor network algorithms, and quantum computing approaches, demonstrating how tensorized Fourier methods can provide scalable alternatives for high-dimensional financial computations.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum Steering and Nonlocal Correlations Between Non-Interacting Delocalized Electrons Under Rashba Spin-Orbit Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28450)
*Zakaria Bouafia,Mostafa Mansour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了二维电子气中两个电子之间的量子导引和非局域关联如何受Rashba自旋轨道相互作用(RSOI)强度和电子间距离的影响,发现在Bi/Ag(111)系统中存在最优RSOI强度(4.32×10^-11 eV·m),可通过调控RSOI强度和电子间距来控制量子关联的抑制与恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维电子气系统(特别是具有强RSOI的Bi/Ag(111)系统)中,量子关联会随电子间距离增加而衰减。研究者需要理解RSOI如何影响量子导引、Bell非局域性和不确定性诱导的非局域性等量子特性,以便找到稳定和调控这些量子资源的方法,这对于量子信息处理和自旋电子学应用具有重要意义。

Method: 研究以Bi/Ag(111)系统为研究对象(其RSOI强度α₀ = 3.05×10^-11 eV·m),系统地分析了三种量子关联度量(Bell非局域性、不确定性诱导的非局域性和量子导引)作为RSOI强度(αᵣ)和电子间距离函数的动力学行为。通过改变RSOI强度参数和电子间分离距离,研究了这些量子关联指标的非单调变化规律。

Result: 研究发现:1)增加αᵣ最初会抑制量子关联;2)所有三种量子关联度量指标均表现出非单调的恢复行为;3)在所考虑的电子间距范围内,这些指标在最优耦合强度αᵣ = 4.32×10^-11 eV·m附近达到峰值;4)RSOI可以作为关键控制参数来稳定量子特性,对抗量子关联随电子间距增加而衰减的趋势。

Conclusion: RSOI是调控二维电子气中量子特性的关键控制参数。在Bi/Ag(111)系统中,可以通过精细调节RSOI强度和电子间距离来控制量子资源(包括Bell非局域性、不确定性诱导的非局域性和量子导引)的抑制与恢复,存在最优RSOI强度使量子关联最大化。这为利用自旋轨道耦合来维持和操控二维材料中的量子关联提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We investigate quantum steering and nonlocal correlations between two electrons in a two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) as functions of Rashba spin-orbit interaction (RSOI) strength and inter-electron separation. We focus particularly on the Bi/Ag(111) system characterized by its strong RSOI ($α_0 = 3.05\times10^{-11}$ eV~m), and we explore the influence of tuning intensity of RSOI and inter-electron distance on the dynamics of Bell nonlocality, uncertainty-induced nonlocality and steering. We find that, although increasing $α_R$ initially suppresses quantum correlations, all three metrics exhibit a non-monotonic recovery as functions of $α_R$, peaking near an optimal coupling strength $α_R = 4.32\times10^{-11}$~eV~m across the range of inter-electron separations considered. This finding establishes RSOI as a critical control parameter for stabilizing quantum properties in two-dimensional electron gases against the decay of quantum correlations with inter-electron separation, and shows that the suppression and recovery of quantum resources within the Bi/Ag(111) system can be controlled by adjusting the inter-electron distance and carefully tuning the RSOI strength.

</details>


### [101] [Mitigating quantum decoherence via global optimal control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28479)
*Ashkan Abedi,Roberto Menta,Julien Despres,Marco Polini,Francesco Caravelli,Vittorio Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究展示了全局最优控制可以显著抑制全局驱动超导量子计算架构中的退相干影响,通过张量网络方法量化了振幅阻尼和退相位信道对量子信息流和量子门操作保真度的影响,并证明通过整形全局驱动可将门序列时间压缩一个数量级,从而恢复高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全局驱动的超导量子处理器中,退相干会作用于每个物理量子比特(包括逻辑寄存器外维持有序相的量子比特),无法通过保护孤立子系统来抑制其影响。因此需要开发新的方法来克服全局驱动架构中退相干对量子门操作保真度和量子信息传输的严重影响。

Method: 采用基于张量网络的方法量化振幅阻尼(amplitude-damping)和退相位(dephasing)信道如何降低准二维梯形几何结构中的量子信息流动以及单量子比特和双量子比特门操作的保真度。通过全局最优控制技术对全局驱动进行时间整形(temporal shaping),优化门操作序列。

Result: 通过整形全局驱动,成功将门操作序列的执行时间压缩了一个数量级,从而恢复了高量子门保真度。这种缓解效果并非通过保护孤立子系统实现,而是纯粹通过全局驱动的时间整形来克服退相干影响。

Conclusion: 全局最优控制通过时间整形技术可以有效抑制全局驱动超导量子计算架构中的退相干影响。这种方法的关键优势在于,即使退相干同时作用于所有物理量子比特(包括支撑有序相的辅助量子比特),仍能通过压缩门操作时间来恢复高保真度,为全局驱动量子处理器的实用化提供了重要的抗噪声策略。

Abstract: We show that global optimal control can drastically suppress the impact of decoherence in globally driven superconducting quantum computing architectures, taking as a prototype a recently proposed quasi-two-dimensional ladder geometry. Using a tensor-network-based approach, we quantify how amplitude-damping and dephasing channels degrade the flow of quantum information along the ladder and the fidelity of one- and two-qubit gate operations. We then demonstrate that shaping the global drive compresses the gate sequences by an order of magnitude in time, restoring high gate fidelities. We stress that this mitigation is far from trivial: in a globally driven processor, dissipation acts on every physical qubit---including those outside the logical register that sustain the surrounding ordered phases---so its impact cannot be suppressed by protecting an isolated subsystem, and is instead overcome purely through the temporal shaping of the global drive.

</details>


### [102] [Optical linewidth narrowing for device-coupled single T centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28485)
*Adam Johnston,Yu-En Wong,Shengbin Yan,Ulises Felix-Rendon,Songtao Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究通过激光扫描显微镜提供的带上光学激发,成功将硅中单个T中心的光学线宽降低了高达70%,为推进T中心自旋量子信息和网络应用平台扫清了重要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中单个T中心作为量子网络应用中具有前景的光学活性自旋,面临的主要障碍是由光谱扩散引起的宽光学线宽,比其腔增强辐射线宽大两个数量级,严重制约了该系统的发展。

Method: 采用激光扫描显微镜向器件耦合的单个T中心提供带上光学激发;利用脉冲带上激发分析电荷稳定动力学;建立速率方程模型来描述动力学过程并解释观察到的线宽变窄和中心频移现象。

Result: 实现了单个T中心光学线宽高达70%的减小;通过光生自由载流子填充附近的电荷陷阱实现了线宽变窄效应;速率方程模型成功描述了电荷稳定动力学及线宽变化规律。

Conclusion: 该研究为控制和减小单个T中心的光学线宽提供了有效途径,扫清了推进单T中心自旋平台在量子信息和网络应用中的主要障碍之一,为该量子系统的实际应用奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Single T centers in silicon have emerged as promising optically active spins for quantum networking applications. One of the major obstacles to advancing the system is their broad optical linewidth due to spectral diffusion, which is two orders of magnitude larger than their cavity-enhanced radiative linewidth. We tackle this issue by utilizing above-band optical excitation delivered via a laser scanning microscope to device-coupled single T centers, achieving up to 70% optical linewidth reduction. We attribute the linewidth narrowing effect to the filling of nearby charge traps by photo-generated free carriers. We analyze charge stabilization dynamics by exploiting pulsed above-band excitation and develop a rate equation model to describe the dynamics and to explain the observed linewidth narrowing and center shift. This work provides an effective pathway to control and reduce the optical linewidth for single T centers, clearing one of the major roadblocks to advance the single T center spin platform for quantum information and networking applications.

</details>


### [103] [Generating broadband optical squeezing via Cascaded Micro-Ring Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28493)
*Chung-Hsien Wang,Tian Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用级联参量微环谐振器阵列生成宽带压缩真空光的可扩展方案,通过将压缩过程分布在多个中等泵浦的谐振器上,克服了传统单腔源在压缩深度和光谱带宽之间的权衡问题,为在集成光子平台上实现实用的压缩光储备库提供了技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单腔压缩光源面临压缩深度与光谱带宽之间的本质权衡,难以同时实现深度压缩和宽带输出。宽带压缩光作为马尔可夫储备库可以指数级增强光-物质相互作用,对量子技术具有重要价值。因此需要开发新的架构来突破单腔限制,在成熟的集成光子平台上实现实用的宽带压缩光源,同时放宽对低品质因子和高增益的苛刻要求。

Method: 提出了一种基于级联参量微环谐振器的可扩展架构,多个微环通过共用总线波导耦合。通过分析输出光谱,确定了在实际腔内泵浦衰减条件下产生宽平顶压缩光谱的具体条件。系统性研究了该架构对制造缺陷(包括非均匀谐振器频率和器件失效)的鲁棒性。理论分析了平顶光谱相比单腔洛伦兹轮廓向马尔可夫极限收敛的速度,并量化了不同谐振器数量和损耗比参数下所需的带宽降低效果。

Result: 仅使用N=5个耦合谐振器且本征损耗比为κ_I/κ=0.1时,相比单腔源,达到相同压缩效果所需的带宽可降低至四分之一。平顶压缩光谱向马尔可夫极限的收敛速度显著快于单腔洛伦兹轮廓。该架构对制造缺陷表现出良好的鲁棒性,即使存在谐振器频率不均匀性和部分器件失效,仍能保持有效的宽带压缩输出。通过将压缩过程分布到多个中等泵浦强度的谐振器,成功放宽了单腔宽带方案对低品质因子和高增益的严格要求。

Conclusion: 级联参量微环谐振器架构为生成宽带压缩真空光提供了一条实用且可扩展的技术路径。该方案通过分布式压缩过程有效解决了传统单腔源的带宽-压缩深度权衡问题,显著降低了对带宽的要求,并展现出对制造缺陷的良好容忍度。这为在成熟的集成光子平台上工程化实现马尔可夫压缩光储备库开辟了新的可能性,有望推动基于压缩光的量子技术应用发展。

Abstract: Broadband squeezed light functioning as a Markovian reservoir can exponentially enhance light-matter interactions, benefiting quantum technologies. However, conventional single-cavity sources face a trade-off between squeezing depth and spectral bandwidth. We propose a scalable scheme for generating broadband squeezed vacuum using a cascade of parametric microring resonators coupled to a common bus waveguide. By analyzing the output, we identify the specific conditions that yield a broad, flat-topped squeezing spectrum, even under realistic intracavity pump attenuation. We demonstrate that this architecture is robust against fabrication imperfections, including inhomogeneous resonator frequencies and component failures. We show that the flat-topped spectrum converges to the Markovian limit significantly faster than a single-cavity Lorentzian profile. An array of as few as $N=5$ coupled resonators with an intrinsic loss ratio of $κ_I/κ= 0.1$ reduces the required bandwidth to a quarter of that needed by a single cavity to achieve same squeezing. This rapid convergence relaxes the low-$Q$ and high-gain constraints of single broadband cavities, distributing the squeezing process across moderately pumped resonators to provide a practical route for engineering squeezed reservoirs on mature integrated photonic platforms.

</details>


### [104] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Simulation of Wave Equations on a Trapped-Ion Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28499)
*Abhishek Shringi,Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Ahmed Shokry,Xiantao Li,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在Quantinuum H2-2离子阱量子处理器上实现了一维和二维声波方程及可变质量Dirac动力学的量子模拟，在多达4096维的编码状态空间中展示了准确的可观测量动力学，为近期量子模拟偏微分方程提供了硬件级验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管波动方程为近期量子模拟偏微分方程提供了天然测试平台，但硬件演示在空间维度、方程类别、系统规模和物理意义输出方面仍然受限。需要开发能够处理更大规模、更复杂物理系统的量子电路，并在实际量子硬件上验证其有效性。

Method: 开发了基于傅里叶变换的保结构量子电路，用于模拟一维和二维声波方程以及可变质量Dirac动力学。在Quantinuum H2-2离子阱处理器上实施实验，处理一维网格最多1024个点和32×32二维网格（编码状态空间维度最高达4096）。不重构完整场，而是直接从测量样本估计子域动能。采用乘积公式方法进行时间演化，分析编译门数随网格量子比特数和演化步数的增长规律。

Result: 在所有测试的声波和Dirac动力学问题中，H2-2处理器的结果与经典动能动力学高度吻合，平均绝对误差在5.9×10^-3到2.4×10^-2之间。在固定保留带宽下，编译门数随网格量子比特数近似二次增长；声波电路规模基本独立于演化时间，而Dirac电路成本还随乘积公式步数增长。成功演示了在当前离子阱处理器上对数千个编码自由度的结构化波动问题进行准确可观测量动力学模拟。

Conclusion: 研究提供了硬件级证据，表明在现有离子阱量子处理器上，对具有数千个编码自由度的结构化波动问题，准确的可观测量动力学仍然可以保持可分辨性。这为量子模拟偏微分方程的实际应用奠定了基础，展示了近期量子设备在处理大规模物理问题方面的潜力，并为未来更复杂的量子模拟应用指明了方向。

Abstract: Wave equations provide a natural testbed for near-term quantum simulation of partial differential equations, but hardware demonstrations have remained limited in spatial dimension, equation class, system size, and physically meaningful output. We develop and benchmark structure-preserving, Fourier-based quantum circuits for the one- and two dimensional acoustic wave equations and Dirac dynamics with variable mass on the Quantinuum H2-2 trapped-ion processor. The experiments include one-dimensional grids with up to \(1024\) points and \(32\times32\) two-dimensional grids, corresponding to an encoded state-space dimension of up to \(4096\). Rather than reconstructing the full fields, we estimate subdomain kinetic energies directly from measurement samples. Across all tested acoustic and Dirac dynamics problems, the H2-2 results track the classical kinetic-energy dynamics with mean absolute errors between \(5.9\times10^{-3}\) and \(2.4\times10^{-2}\). At fixed retained bandwidth, the compiled gate counts grow approximately quadratically with the number of grid qubits; the acoustic circuit sizes are essentially independent of evolution time, whereas the cost also grows with the number of product-formula steps. These results provide hardware-level evidence that accurate observable dynamics can remain resolvable for structured wave problems with thousands of encoded degrees of freedom on a present-day trapped-ion processor.

</details>


### [105] [Spacetime Layout and Logical Compilation of Color Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28504)
*Qinjing Yu,Ke Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了颜色码(color code)的逻辑编译框架,通过时空块图表示和ZX图的对应关系,开发了代码衍生的编译策略,实现了从逻辑计算到颜色码时空布局的自动化转换,将颜色码架构从单个原语推进到逻辑计算的自动化综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要系统级的逻辑原语协调。颜色码作为一种拓扑量子纠错码,需要从单个逻辑操作原语层面发展到完整的逻辑计算自动化综合层面,以实现全栈量子计算。现有研究缺乏针对颜色码的系统性逻辑编译框架,无法高效地将抽象逻辑计算转换为具体的颜色码时空实现。

Method: 1. 基于任意子凝聚(anyon-condensation)和畴壁(domain-wall)结构,引入时空块图(spacetime block-diagram)表示来捕获逻辑补丁和操作;2. 推导块组装(block assembly)的约束规则;3. 建立与ZX图的对应关系,识别逻辑语义并实现保持计算等价的变换;4. 开发代码衍生的编译策略,使用边修饰的ZX图(edge-decorated ZX diagrams)适配颜色码的块组装约束;5. 采用融合区域感知路由(fusion-region-aware routing)利用语义等价性进行几何嵌入;6. 实现完整逻辑编译流程的自动化。

Result: 成功实现了逻辑编译过程的完全自动化,并在广泛的算法范围内展示了成功的编译结果。该框架能够将ZX表示的逻辑计算有效转换为有效的颜色码时空布局,验证了所提出方法的实用性和通用性。

Conclusion: 本研究将颜色码架构从单个原语操作推进到逻辑计算的自动化综合,标志着向全栈量子计算迈出了重要一步。建立的逻辑编译框架为颜色码的系统级实现提供了完整的理论和技术支撑,使得容错量子计算中的逻辑原语能够实现系统级协调,为颜色码在实际量子计算中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires system-level coordination of logical primitives. Here, we establish a logical compilation framework for the color code, grounded in its topological structure and supporting universal logical operations. Based on its anyon-condensation and domain-wall structure, we introduce a spacetime block-diagram representation capturing logical patches and operations and derive the rules governing block assembly. A correspondence with ZX diagrams further identifies the logical semantics of this representation and enables transformations that preserve the represented computation. Moreover, we develop a code-derived compilation strategy that converts ZX representations of logical computations into valid color-code spacetime layouts. In this strategy, edge-decorated ZX diagrams tailor the logical representation to the color code under the block-assembly constraints, and fusion-region-aware routing exploits semantic equivalence during geometric embedding. We automate the complete logical compilation process and demonstrate successful compilation across a broad range of algorithms. Our work advances color-code architecture from individual primitives to the automated synthesis of logical computations, marking a significant step toward its full-stack quantum computing.

</details>


### [106] [Spectral gaps of ironed two-qubit gadgets matching the iSWAP gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28521)
*Yanying Liang,Haoran Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在完全图上，满足特定参数条件(a=5/9)的熨烫双量子比特门电路与iSWAP门电路具有相同的二阶矩谱间隙，通过表示论局部化定理解决了Kong、Li和Liu的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Kong、Li和Liu提出的关于特定参数熨烫双量子比特门电路谱性质的猜想，探究不同量子门电路在完全图拓扑结构上的谱间隙等价性问题，这对理解量子电路的混合时间和优化性能具有重要理论意义。

Method: 核心方法是建立表示论局部化定理，证明关联的对称群不变算子的最大严格负特征值总是出现在最高自旋SU(2)子空间中。通过构造局部正半定分解，将除最高两个自旋扇区之外的所有自旋扇区分离开来，从而精确刻画谱结构。

Result: 成功证明了在顶点数n≥5的完全图Kn上，KAK分解参数满足a=5/9的任意熨烫双量子比特门电路与iSWAP门电路具有相同的二阶矩谱间隙。证明过程确认了最大严格负特征值定位于最高自旋表示中，并通过正半定分解技术有效分离了不同自旋扇区。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Kong、Li和Liu的猜想，揭示了特定参数族的熨烫双量子比特门电路与iSWAP门电路在谱性质上的深层等价关系。表示论局部化定理和正半定分解方法为研究量子电路的谱结构提供了有力的数学工具，对量子算法设计和量子行走分析具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We prove that every ironed two-qubit gadget whose KAK-derived parameter satisfies $a=5/9$ has, on the complete graph $K_n$ with $n\geqslant 5$, the same second-moment spectral gap as the iSWAP gadget. The central step is a representation-theoretic localisation theorem: the largest strictly negative eigenvalue of the associated $\mathfrak S_n$-invariant operator always occurs in the highest-spin $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ summand. A local positive-semidefinite decomposition separates every spin sector except the two highest. This settles a conjecture of Kong, Li, and Liu.

</details>


### [107] [When quantum thermal states look classical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28536)
*Harald Putterman,Alexander Zlokapa,Jordan Cotler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子吉布斯态在有限温度下的经典-量子转变层次结构,证明了纠缠消失、魔性消失、配分函数解析性、关联衰减等性质在不同的逆温度尺度下失效,并为长程泡利相互作用系统提供了多项式时间经典算法来制备吉布斯态和估计热期望值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高温下,量子吉布斯态保留了最大混合态的若干经典特征(无纠缠、无魔性、配分函数解析性、关联衰减和算法可处理性)。然而,这些特征在何种有限温度下持续存在、又在何种温度尺度下失效,形成怎样的经典-量子转变层次结构,是一个尚未清楚解决的开放问题。特别是对于长程相互作用系统,纠缠消失的温度是否独立于系统尺寸是Rouze等人提出的重要开放问题,且现有算法的适用温度范围与物理转变温度之间存在gap需要填补。

Method: 研究方法包括:(1)针对每个格点上强度有界的长程泡利相互作用系统,建立新的尖锐边界来刻画不同量子特征在有限温度下的行为;(2)通过严格数学证明确定纠缠消失、魔性消失、关联衰减等性质失效的精确逆温度尺度;(3)设计多项式时间经典算法,用于在纠缠消失转变温度之前制备吉布斯态;(4)证明在更低温度下吉布斯态仍处于热力学无限温度相,从而可以有效估计热期望值;(5)将分析扩展到全对全相互作用情况,解决常数温度下纠缠消失的问题。

Result: (1)证明了纠缠在常数温度下消失,且该温度独立于系统尺寸,解决了Rouze等人的开放问题;(2)改进了Bakshi等人的可分性温度界,使其达到常数因子最优;(3)构造的经典算法可在渐近更低的温度下工作,超越了已知量子吉布斯采样器快速混合的温度范围;(4)在存在纠缠和魔性的更低温度下,仍能用多项式时间经典算法估计热期望值;(5)解决了Harrow等人提出的关联衰减猜想;(6)建立了经典-量子转变的完整层次结构,显示不同量子特征在不同逆温度尺度下失效。

Conclusion: 本文系统地揭示了量子吉布斯态从高温经典行为到低温量子行为的多层次转变结构。证明了对于长程泡利相互作用系统,尽管存在全对全相互作用,纠缠消失仍发生在与系统尺寸无关的常数温度,并给出了紧的温度界。更重要的是,不同量子特征(纠缠、魔性、关联衰减)在不同温度尺度下失效,形成清晰的层次结构。研究还表明经典算法的能力边界可以延伸到比之前认为的更低温度,即使在存在量子纠缠和魔性的情况下仍可高效计算热性质。这些结果不仅解决了多个重要的开放问题,也为理解量子多体系统的热化行为和设计高效量子算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: At high temperature, quantum Gibbs states retain several classical features of the maximally mixed state: the absence of entanglement, the absence of magic, analyticity of the partition function, correlation decay, and algorithmic tractability. We prove new and sharp bounds showing that these features persist down to finite temperatures independent of system size, but fail at distinct inverse-temperature scales, forming a hierarchy of classical-to-quantum transitions. Our results hold for long-range Pauli interactions with bounded strength at every site. Despite such all-to-all interactions, we show that the death of entanglement occurs at constant temperature, resolving an open question of Rouze, Franca and Alhambra (STOC'25). We give a polynomial-time classical algorithm that prepares Gibbs states up to the death of entanglement transition. Notably, this is asymptotically colder than temperatures at which quantum Gibbs samplers are known to mix quickly, as well as the original separability temperature of Bakshi et al. (FOCS'24), which we improve to be tight up to constants. At asymptotically even colder temperatures, we show that the Gibbs state remains in the thermodynamic infinite-temperature phase. This leads to polynomial-time classical algorithms for estimating thermal expectations despite both entanglement and magic, and the resolution of a correlation decay conjecture of Harrow, Mehraban and Soleimanifar (STOC'20).

</details>


### [108] [A Provable Oracle-Free Quantum Algorithm for Nonlinear Dynamics on Hybrid Oscillator-Qubit Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28541)
*Kausthubh Chandramouli,Yan Li,Yuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子比特-量子模式算法,用于求解多项式漂移的非线性常微分方程。该算法通过Fokker-Planck方法传播状态密度,利用扭曲相位变换将离散化生成器转化为参数化Schrödinger方程族,并将傅里叶模式参数放置在单个连续变量量子模式上,实现了$\mathcal{O}(d^{L+1}n^{L+2})$门复杂度且无需稀疏访问预言机的高效量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解非线性常微分方程在科学计算中具有重要意义,但经典方法在高维或复杂系统中计算成本高昂。现有量子算法往往依赖稀疏访问预言机或块编码等复杂结构,且对连续变量系统的处理不够高效。因此,需要开发一种无需这些复杂结构、能直接由漂移多项式系数确定所有门操作、并能充分利用连续变量量子模式优势的新型量子算法。

Method: 算法采用Fokker-Planck路径传播状态密度,在小噪声极限下从密度峰值恢复确定性轨迹。通过扭曲相位变换将离散化生成器转化为参数化Schrödinger方程族,将傅里叶模式参数编码到单个连续变量量子模式上。核心结构贡献是证明了离散化生成器的厄米部分$H_{1}$和$H_{2}$具有二分Pauli分解:将非零Pauli串分类为$\mathcal{O}(\log N)$个互相对易的族,每个族因式分解为至多$L$次的对角算子与固定秩二键算子的张量积。这种因式分解使每个族指数精确表示为$\mathcal{O}(n^{L})$个单项式控制动量位移的乘积,族内无Trotter误差。

Result: 在每轴$N=2^{n}$个网格点的$d$维网格上,电路每个Trotter步需要$\mathcal{O}(d^{L+1}n^{L+2})$个门。所有门操作均由漂移的多项式系数以闭式形式确定,无需调用稀疏访问预言机或块编码。证明了数值横坐标$λ_{\max}(H_{1})$的界,固定了扭曲相位变换的恢复域和后选择成本。对两个非线性基准问题的经典模拟验证了结构定理、移位恢复特性,以及连续变量耦合相对于离散化模式寄存器在精度-资源比方面的优势。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了一种高效的混合量子比特-量子模式算法用于求解多项式漂移非线性常微分方程,通过二分Pauli分解和因式分解结构实现了低门复杂度且无族内Trotter误差的量子电路。算法完全由漂移多项式系数闭式确定,避免了复杂的量子预言机结构。理论分析和数值实验均证实了该方法的有效性,特别是连续变量量子模式在精度和资源效率方面的显著优势,为量子计算求解非线性动力系统提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid qubit--qumode algorithm for nonlinear ordinary differential equations of the form $\dot{\mathbf{x}}=\mathbf{f}(\mathbf{x})$ with drift of polynomial degree~$L$. Following the Fokker--Planck route of Tennie and Magri, the algorithm propagates the state density and returns the deterministic trajectory as the peak of that density in the small-noise limit. The discretised generator is carried into a parametrised family of Schrödinger equations by the warped-phase transformation of Jin, Liu, and Yu, and the Fourier-mode parameter of that family is placed on a single continuous-variable qumode. Our central structural result is that the Hermitian parts $H_{1}$ and $H_{2}$ of the discretised generator admit a bipartite Pauli decomposition that sorts the non-zero Pauli strings into $\mathcal{O}(\log N)$ mutually commuting families and factorises each family into a diagonal of degree at most $L$ tensored with a fixed rank-two bond operator. The factorisation renders each family exponential an exact product of $\mathcal{O}(n^{L})$ monomial-controlled momentum displacements, with no intra-family Trotter error. On a $d$-dimensional grid of $N=2^{n}$ points per axis the circuit costs $\mathcal{O}(d^{L+1}n^{L+2})$ gates per Trotter step. No sparse-access oracle and no block encoding is invoked: every gate is fixed in closed form by the polynomial coefficients of the drift. We also prove a bound on the numerical abscissa $λ_{\max}(H_{1})$ that fixes the recovery domain of the warped-phase transform and the post-selection cost. A classical simulation on two nonlinear benchmarks confirms the structural theorems, the shifted recovery, and the accuracy-per-resource advantage of the continuous-variable coupling over a discretised mode register.

</details>


### [109] [Statistically secure uncloneable encryption of arbitrary messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28561)
*Archishna Bhattacharyya,Anne Broadbent,Eric Culf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将单比特不可克隆加密方案扩展到任意长度消息,利用Clifford酉算子子集的编码基础,实现了具有统计安全性的任意长度消息一次性不可克隆加密,编码时间关于消息长度和安全参数呈多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单比特不可克隆加密已实现高效加密和解密,但能否将其扩展到任意长度消息并保持统计安全性仍是未知问题。研究者希望突破这一限制,建立任意长度消息的无条件不可克隆加密方案,并证明其统计安全性。

Method: 利用已知安全的单比特方案的编码基由Clifford酉算子子集构成这一关键性质,将该方案升级扩展至任意长度消息。通过设计编码时间关于消息长度和安全参数呈多项式级别的方案,实现统计安全的不可克隆加密。

Result: 成功构建了适用于任意长度消息的无条件不可克隆加密方案,编码和解密时间均为多项式级别(相对于消息长度和安全参数),并实现了统计安全性保证。

Conclusion: 证明了任意长度消息的一次性不可克隆加密可以达到统计安全性。这一结果确立了不可克隆加密从单比特到任意长度消息的可扩展性,为量子密码学中的不可克隆加密理论提供了重要的安全性保障。

Abstract: Unconditional uncloneable encryption of a single bit with efficient encryption and decryption is now possible. However, whether the extension to messages of arbitrary length achieves statistical security remains to be known. Using the fact that the encoding bases for the single-bit scheme known to be secure consist of a subset of the Clifford unitaries, we show that this scheme can be upgraded to achieve unconditional uncloneable encryption for messages of arbitrary length, with encoding time polynomial in the message length and security parameter. This establishes that one-time uncloneable encryption of arbitrary messages enjoys statistical security.

</details>


### [110] [Benchmarking Quantum Simulations of the Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick Model Using Large Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28570)
*Maggie Bao,Rushil Dandamudi,Jerimiah Wright,Joan Étude Arrow,Henry Zou,Vardaan Sahgal,Brian J. McDermott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)算法计算Lipkin Meshkov Glick(LMG)模型的基态能量,与变分量子本征求解器(VQE)和基于采样的量子对角化(SQD)等NISQ算法进行性能基准对比,发现在噪声中等规模量子时代,基于子空间的方法在精度、电路深度和噪声鲁棒性之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算技术的成熟,迫切需要将其实际问题求解性能与具有竞争力的经典方法(如张量网络)进行基准测试对比。本文旨在通过经典DMRG算法为NISQ量子算法提供高精度的基准数据集,评估当前量子计算在实际问题求解中的性能表现。

Method: 利用密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)算法在NERSC Perlmutter超级计算机上计算LMG模型的基态能量,生成包含多达1400个粒子系统的大规模高精度数据集。将这些结果与在IBM Eagle量子计算机上实现的VQE和SQD算法进行对比分析,评估不同方法在精度、电路深度和噪声鲁棒性方面的表现。

Result: 研究提供了文献中最大的LMG基态能量数据集之一,包含多达1400个粒子系统的精确基态能量。对比实验显示:VQE在6个粒子时达到1%误差以内的精度,但在其他粒子数下均超过该阈值;SQD将高精度范围扩展到17个粒子,表现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: 在噪声中等规模量子(NISQ)时代,基于子空间的方法(如SQD)在精度、电路深度和噪声鲁棒性之间取得了最佳平衡,相比传统VQE方法能够处理更大规模的系统并保持较高精度。研究为量子算法与经典算法的性能对比提供了重要的基准数据,揭示了当前量子计算技术在实际应用中的能力边界。

Abstract: As quantum computing matures, it is critical to benchmark its real-world problem solving performance against competitive classical methods, such as tensor networks. In this work, we leverage the Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) algorithm to compute ground state energies of the Lipkin Meshkov Glick (LMG) model as a comparative benchmark against popular noisy intermediate-scale (NISQ) algorithms like the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) method. By running DMRG on the NERSC Perlmutter supercomputer, we provide one of the largest LMG ground state energy datasets in literature, containing accurate ground state energies for systems up to 1400 particles. We compare these results with VQE and SQD implementations on an IBM Eagle quantum computer for comparison. VQE achieved results within 1 percent error for 6 particles, while exceeding that threshold for all other values while SQD extended that range to 17 particles, suggesting that in a noisy intermediate scale quantum era, subspace-based approaches may strike the best balance between accuracy, circuit depth, and noise resilience.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Fidelity-per-Cost: A Metric for Evaluation of Quantum Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28572)
*Siddarth Shinde,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对14个云端量子处理器(QPU)进行了跨平台的执行保真度测量研究,并提出了一种成本感知评估指标"量子保真度-成本比"(QFC),研究发现基于成本的量子计算机排名与纯保真度排名存在差异,计费模式而非硬件特性主导了评分随测量次数的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云端量子计算的普及,量子计算机的比较不仅是物理基准测试,更成为实际的采购决策问题。然而,在各云服务提供商异构计费模式下,考虑成本的量子计算机比较研究仍然不足且难以实施,因此需要开发一种能够综合考虑性能和价格的评估方法。

Method: 本研究采用跨平台测量方法,对四个云访问路径(AWS、IBM Quantum Runtime、IQM Resonance和OQC)上的14个QPU访问入口(12个不同的物理QPU)进行量子电路执行保真度测量。提出了量子保真度-成本比(QFC)评分指标,该指标将Kullback-Leibler(KL)散度(衡量与理想输出分布的偏差)、测量次数(shot count)和货币成本整合到一个统一的度量标准中,并在文档化的计费模型下进行分析。

Result: 主要实证观察表明:1)基于成本的量子计算机排名与纯粹基于保真度的评估结果存在差异;2)用户在考虑价格因素时可能会选择不同的量子计算后端,而非仅基于保真度选择;3)该排名指标在重新加权后保持稳定;4)设备的计费模式(而非硬件本身)决定了其评分随测量次数的缩放规律;5)QFC值会随着新设备上线或提供商价格调整而变化。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了在量子计算机选择中引入成本考量的重要性和必要性。成本感知评估可以改变量子计算机的排名顺序,为用户提供更符合实际应用场景的决策依据。计费模式对性能-成本权衡的影响超过硬件差异本身,这为量子计算云服务的定价策略和用户选择提供了重要参考。该研究为量子计算商业化应用中的资源选择提供了实用的评估框架。

Abstract: Cloud-accessible quantum computing has made hardware comparison not only a physics benchmark but also a practical purchasing decision. Cost-aware comparison of quantum computers remains underexplored and is difficult to do under the heterogeneous billing models offered by various cloud-based quantum computing providers. This paper makes two main contributions to enable price-aware comparison of quantum computers. First, this work presents a cross-provider measurement study of quantum circuit execution fidelity spanning 14 cloud QPU access-path entries (12 distinct physical QPUs) across four cloud access paths: Amazon Web Services (AWS) cloud, IBM Quantum Runtime (IBM) cloud, IQM Resonance (IQM) cloud, and Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC) cloud. Second, this work proposes and analyzes a cost-aware score, Quantum Fidelity-per-Cost (QFC), which combines Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence from an ideal output distribution, shot count, and monetary cost into one possible metric under a documented billing model. The main empirical observation from this work is that cost-aware ranking can differ from purely fidelity-based evaluation of quantum computers, and that users may select different quantum computing backends when they consider price in their selection, as opposed to selection based on fidelity alone. This work shows that the ranking is stable under reweighting of the metric, and that a device's billing model, not its hardware, governs how its score scales with shot count. Reported QFC values change as new machines come online or as providers revise their prices.

</details>


### [112] [Explicit Matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$ with CNOT and Row Complexity $4n-\mathrm{o}(n)$ and Local Logic Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28598)
*Sherry Gong,Andrew Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族显式的 n×n 二元可逆矩阵,证明其 CNOT 门复杂度和行复杂度至少为 4n-o(n),并将局部逻辑门生成的置换群与二元域上仿射变换群建立同构关系,从而将量子复杂度问题归约为矩阵行化简复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路综合中的一个核心问题是确定实现特定量子操作所需的基本门(如 CNOT 门)的最少数量。虽然已知存在需要高复杂度的矩阵,但缺乏显式构造的例子。本文旨在构造显式的困难实例矩阵,并建立局部逻辑门置换群与仿射变换群之间的联系,从而为量子复杂度研究提供理论工具和具体下界。

Method: 1. 显式构造:设计了一族特定的 n×n 二元可逆矩阵,具有可分析的结构特性;
2. 复杂度下界证明:通过组合分析和代数方法,严格证明这些矩阵的 CNOT 复杂度和行化简复杂度至少为 4n-o(n);
3. 群论刻画:定义由作用于 n 位二进制串的局部逻辑门生成的置换群 G_n,证明 G_n 与二元向量空间 Z_2^n 上所有可逆仿射变换群自然同构;
4. 复杂度归约:利用上述同构关系,将 G_n 中置换的量子复杂度问题归约为 Z_2 上可逆矩阵的行化简复杂度问题。

Result: 1. 给出了首个显式构造的 n×n 二元可逆矩阵族,其 CNOT 复杂度和行复杂度下界为 4n-o(n);
2. 证明了即使使用更强的计算模型(任意局部线性逻辑门代替 CNOT 门),该复杂度下界依然成立;
3. 建立了局部逻辑门生成的置换群 G_n 与 Z_2^n 上可逆仿射变换群的自然同构;
4. 作为应用,证明了与所构造矩阵相关的置换具有至少 4n-o(n) 的量子复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式构造证明了存在需要近似线性数量(4n-o(n))基本门的二元可逆矩阵,这一结果在更强的局部线性逻辑门模型下仍然成立。更重要的是,通过建立局部逻辑门置换群与仿射变换群的同构关系,将量子复杂度研究归约为经典的矩阵行化简问题,为后续研究提供了重要的理论框架和分析工具,推进了对量子电路复杂度下界的理解。

Abstract: In this article, we present an explicit family of invertible $n\times n$ matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$ whose CNOT and row complexity is at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$; equivalently, reducing these matrices to the identity requires at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$ elementary row operations. Moreover, the same complexity lower bound holds in the stronger computational model where the CNOT gates are replaced by arbitrary local linear logic gates, namely arbitrary invertible linear transformations acting on pairs of coordinates.
  Let $G_n$ denote the permutation group generated by local logic gates acting on the set of binary strings of length $n$. We prove that $G_n$ is naturally isomorphic to the group of all invertible affine transformations of the vector space $\mathbb Z_2^n$, thus reducing the problem of estimating the quantum complexity of permutations in $G_n$ to the row reduction complexity of invertible matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$. As an application, we show that the permutations associated with our explicit matrices have quantum complexity at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$.

</details>


### [113] [SymFT: Universal Fault-Tolerant Quantum Circuit Simulation via Symbolic Clifford--Pauli Frames and Stabilizer Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28600)
*Wang Fang,Huazhe Lou,Riling Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SymFT是一个高吞吐量的量子电路模拟器,专门针对以Clifford操作为主并包含少量非Clifford操作的容错协议。通过符号化Clifford-Pauli框架分解和自适应稳定子坐标规划两项技术,在单CPU核心上相比现有模拟器(Stim和Clifft)实现了2-3倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子协议主要由稳定子子电路组成,但实现通用量子计算所需的非Clifford操作使得精确采样计算成本高昂。现有模拟器在处理Clifford主导型电路(包含Pauli旋转、随机Pauli噪声、中间测量和反馈)时效率不足,需要开发更高效的模拟方法来加速这类近Clifford电路的采样过程。

Method: SymFT结合了两个核心技术:(1)符号化Clifford-Pauli框架分解:将分支概率采样简化为Pauli旋转和测量投影算子,用符号符号表示噪声和反馈。由于残余的Clifford和Pauli框架是幺正的,不影响分支概率,因此无需在每次采样中应用。(2)自适应稳定子坐标规划:使用共享的稳定子-去稳定子表来定义基,仅在动态调整大小的稠密活跃态矢量中存储活跃的非稳定子自由度。该方法一次性解决基变换并生成直接的多坐标采样指令,避免了每次采样时的表更新和局域化引起的Clifford变换。

Result: 在测试的纯Clifford和近Clifford电路中,SymFT实现了最先进的采样性能。在单CPU核心上:对于表面码电路,比Stim快2.51-2.56倍;对于魔术态培养和蒸馏电路,比Clifft快1.86-3.51倍。对于测试的培养电路,其采样吞吐量比作者之前的模拟器SOFT高出两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: SymFT通过符号化框架分解和自适应坐标规划两项创新技术,成功解决了Clifford主导型量子电路模拟的效率瓶颈,在多种容错协议电路上实现了显著的性能提升,为大规模容错量子计算模拟提供了更高效的工具。该模拟器在保持精确采样的同时,大幅降低了计算开销,特别适用于包含少量非Clifford操作的实际容错量子协议。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant protocols often consist largely of stabilizer subcircuits, yet the non-Clifford operations required for universality make exact sampling costly. We present SymFT, a high-throughput simulator for Clifford-dominated circuits with Pauli rotations, stochastic Pauli noise, mid-circuit Pauli measurements, and measurement-record-controlled Pauli feedback. It combines two ideas. First, symbolic Clifford--Pauli frame factorization reduces branch-probability sampling to Pauli rotations and measurement projectors, with noise and feedback represented by symbolic signs. Since the residual Clifford and Pauli frames are unitary, they do not affect branch probabilities and need not be applied in every shot. Second, adaptive stabilizer-coordinate planning uses a shared stabilizer--destabilizer tableau to define the basis and stores only the active non-stabilizer degrees of freedom in a dynamically sized dense active-state vector. It resolves basis changes once and emits direct multi-coordinate sampling instructions, thereby avoiding per-shot tableau updates and localization-induced Clifford transformations of the dense vector. Across the tested pure-Clifford and near-Clifford circuits, SymFT achieves state-of-the-art sampling performance. On a single CPU core, it is $2.51\text{--}2.56\times$ faster than Stim for surface-code circuits and $1.86\text{--}3.51\times$ faster than Clifft for magic-state cultivation and distillation circuits. For the tested cultivation circuits, its sampling throughput also exceeds that of our previous simulator, SOFT, by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [114] [Pauli Encodings & Unclonable Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28602)
*Pierre Botteron,Sébastien Designolle,Omar Fawzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子加密中的不可克隆比特问题,提出了Pauli编码方案,证明了其单配性下界为1/2+1/(2√K),并通过半定规划松弛和数值计算分析了其不可克隆安全性的边界与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子加密能否防止攻击者在密钥泄露后产生两个都能解密明文的系统(不可克隆比特问题),需要理解和建立可证明安全的量子加密方案,特别是针对Pauli编码这类简单但具有代表性的加密协议。

Method: 引入Pauli编码方案(密文为Pauli字符串本征空间投影算子),通过纠缠单配性分析证明通用下界;利用半定规划(SDP)松弛的第三层求解渐近上界;针对受限局部维度攻击者和特定Pauli族证明强不可克隆不可区分安全性;使用NPA层级计算提供数值证据。

Result: 证明了K个Pauli字符串编码的通用下界为1/2+1/(2√K);发现仅使用X和Z的n长度Pauli字符串不安全;识别出基于成对猜测边缘分布的论证存在3/4的通用障碍;对于两两反对易的Pauli字符串协议,通过三层SDP松弛获得约0.5556的渐近获胜概率上界;针对有界局部维度攻击者证明了强安全性。

Conclusion: Pauli编码为研究不可克隆加密提供了可分析的框架,但自然方法存在固有局限性:仅用X和Z不足以保证安全,基于成对边缘分布的分析方法受3/4障碍限制。尽管如此,对特定结构(两两反对易Pauli字符串)和受限攻击者模型下可证明安全性,SDP和NPA数值结果为强不可克隆不可区分安全性提供了积极证据。

Abstract: The unclonable bit question asks whether quantum encryption can prevent an adversary from producing two systems that both reveal the plaintext once the key is disclosed. We introduce and study Pauli Encodings, a simple class of one-bit encryption schemes whose ciphertexts are normalized eigenspace projectors of Pauli strings. For every Pauli Encoding with K Pauli strings, we prove a universal lower bound $1/2+1/(2\sqrt{K})$ on the optimal monogamy-of-entanglement winning probability, together with sharper bounds for several structured families. We then establish two limitations of natural approaches to unclonable security. First, if the Pauli strings are restricted to strings of X and Z of length n, the encoding is not secure. Second, we identify a universal 3/4 obstruction showing that arguments based only on pairwise guessing marginals cannot establish unclonable-indistinguishable security. When the Pauli strings all pairwise anticommute, the protocol becomes the one studied in [Quantum 10, 2157 (2026)]. We exploit the symmetry of this protocol to solve the third level of the natural semidefinite programming relaxation obtaining an asymptotic upper bound of approximately 0.5556 on the winning probability. Finally, we prove strong unclonable-indistinguishable security against bounded-local-dimension adversaries and strong indistinguishability security for several efficient Pauli families. First-level NPA computations provide additional numerical evidence towards the strong unclonable-indistinguishable security.

</details>


### [115] [Logical computation with canonical lifted product codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28605)
*Han Zheng,Guo Zheng,Liang Jiang,Qian Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高码率量子LDPC码提出了一种协同设计方案,通过引入规范逻辑基础和利用循环对称性,实现了高效、模块化的容错量子计算,大幅降低了物理量子比特开销和操作复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率量子LDPC码虽然能以低物理量子比特开销编码多个逻辑量子比特,但在这种密集编码上实现高效容错计算仍面临重大挑战。现有的通用技术(如码手术和门隐形传态)难以在复杂高码率码上实现模块化、低开销和完全可验证的操作,因为这些方法未充分利用码的内在结构特性。

Method: 针对具有循环对称性的规范提升乘积(lifted-product, LP)码族,协同设计码结构及其逻辑指令集。核心方法包括:(1)构建规范逻辑基础,将共轭逻辑算符组织成继承自经典码的循环轨道行列结构;(2)基于该基础开发完整逻辑指令集,包括常数深度自同构门、折叠横贯Clifford门、由少量可复用种子手术工具构建的模块化图码手术、规范提取器、高度并行的逻辑Pauli乘积测量以及并行魔态注入;(3)利用码的循环对称性简化操作并降低资源需求。

Result: 对于[[1122,148,≤20]]的LP码,仅需2个种子手术工具;对于[[4350,1224,≤20]]的LP码,仅需4个种子手术工具。任意高权重逻辑测量可通过小于数据码块一半大小的完整提取器实现。该方案成功实现了模块化、低开销且可验证的容错量子计算操作,显著提高了高码率量子架构的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过将码结构与逻辑操作协同设计,本研究为具有循环对称性的规范提升乘积码提供了完整且高效的容错计算解决方案,推动了超高码率量子架构上容错量子计算的前沿发展,为实现低物理开销、高逻辑密度的量子计算系统奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes encode many logical qubits with low physical-qubit overhead, but realizing efficient fault-tolerant computation on such dense encodings remains a major challenge. Generic, code-agnostic techniques such as code surgery and gate teleportation apply broadly, but are difficult to make modular, low-overhead, and fully certifiable on complex high-rate codes whose structure is left unexploited. Here we overcome these obstacles by co-designing the code together with its logical instruction set for a broad family of \emph{canonical} lifted-product (LP) codes with cyclic symmetry. We show that these codes admit a \emph{canonical logical basis}, in which conjugate logical operators are organized into rows and columns of cyclic orbits inherited directly from the underlying classical codes, analogous to the structure that makes hypergraph-product codes so tractable. This canonical basis unlocks a complete logical instruction set, including constant-depth automorphism and fold-transversal Clifford gates, modular graph code surgeries built from a constant number of reusable seed surgery gadgets or a compact canonical extractor, highly parallel logical Pauli-product measurements, and parallel magic-state injection. For example, a $[[1122,148,\leq\!20]]$ (resp. $[[4350,1224,\leq\!20]]$) LP code requires only two (resp. four) seed surgery gadgets, while arbitrary high-weight logical measurements can be implemented using a full extractor smaller than half of the data code block. These results advance the frontier of fault-tolerant quantum computation on ultra-high-rate quantum architectures.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Arbitrary Lindbladians from Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28610)
*Zhili Chen,Zhan Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效算法,可以从物理时间演化中学习未知的马尔可夫开放系统生成器(Lindbladian),该算法在实验次数和总演化时间上达到近乎最优,仅相差对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习未知的Lindbladian生成器面临指数级大的Pauli项族挑战。现有方法在处理任意Lindbladian时缺乏高效性和最优性,需要开发一种能够在最小假设下、以近乎最优的资源消耗学习任意Lindbladian的算法,包括哈密顿量系数和耗散系数。

Method: 算法包含两个非自适应、无辅助比特、无控制的阶段:1) 支集学习阶段:使用乘积Pauli本征态制备和单量子比特Pauli测量,通过$\widetilde O(Λ^2/η^2)$次实验输出大小为$\mathrm{poly}(Λ/η)$的候选支集,包含所有幅度至少为$η$的哈密顿量和耗散坐标;2) 系数学习阶段:使用随机稳定子态制备和随机Clifford基测量,通过$\widetilde O(Λ^2\log M/ε^{2})$次实验将大小为$M$的候选支集中的所有系数估计到误差$ε$。两阶段组合实现多项式时间内识别和估计任意Lindbladian的每个系数。

Result: 对于动力学强度最大为$Λ$的Lindbladian,算法使用$\widetilde O(Λ^2/ε^2)$次实验和$\widetilde O(Λ/ε^2)$总演化时间,将每个系数估计到误差$ε$,经典运行时间为多项式级别。实验次数和总演化时间的缩放关系与下界仅相差对数因子,达到近乎最优性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了在最小假设下学习任意Lindbladian的问题,提出的两阶段算法在资源消耗上达到近乎最优(与理论下界仅相差对数因子),且无需辅助量子比特或量子控制,具有很强的实用性。该工作为量子开放系统的表征提供了高效的学习框架。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning an unknown Markovian open-system generator from access to its physical time evolution. This generator, called a Lindbladian, contains Hamiltonian and dissipative coefficients indexed by an exponentially large family of possible Pauli terms. We propose an efficient algorithm that learns arbitrary Lindbladians from time evolution under minimal assumptions. For a Lindbladian of dynamical strength at most $Λ$, the algorithm estimates every coefficient to error $ε$ using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/ε^2)$ experiments and $\widetilde O(Λ/ε^2)$ total evolution time, together with polynomial classical running time. The algorithm consists of two nonadaptive, ancilla-free, and control-free stages: 1. The support-learning stage outputs a candidate support of size $\mathrm{poly}(Λ/η)$ that contains every Hamiltonian and dissipative coordinate of magnitude at least $η$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/η^2)$ experiments with preparations of product Pauli eigenstates and single-qubit Pauli measurements. 2.The coefficient-learning stage estimates all coefficients in any candidate support of size $M$ to error $ε$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2\log M/ε^{2})$ experiments with preparations of random stabilizer states and measurements in random Clifford bases. Composing the two stages identifies and estimates every coefficient of an arbitrary Lindbladian in polynomial time. The experiment-count and total-evolution-time scalings match the lower bounds up to logarithmic factors, so the algorithm is nearly optimal for learning arbitrary Lindbladians.

</details>


### [117] [On Optimal Measurement-State Preparation via Geometric Transport of the Squeezing Ellipse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28620)
*Vsevolod Salakhutdinov,Nikolay Kalinin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架，用于在SU(2)对称系统中将初始未对齐的压缩态转换为测量最优态，该框架揭示了压缩椭圆旋转与路径几何及几何相位的内在联系，并以偏振压缩光为例探讨了实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子精密测量中，制备测量最优的压缩态是关键要求。然而，初始制备的压缩态往往与测量最优方向存在失配。需要一种系统性的方法将失配的压缩输入态转换为测量最优态，同时理解这一转换过程的几何本质。

Method: 提出了一种几何框架来处理SU(2)对称系统中的压缩态优化问题。该方法将压缩椭圆的取向视为额外的几何自由度，当平均态沿单位球面上的受控轨迹演化时，压缩椭圆随之旋转。椭圆的旋转由路径的几何性质决定，对于相关的变换类别，旋转角度取决于轨迹所包围的立体角，从而建立了与几何相位的联系。以偏振压缩光为具体案例，提出使用连续变化的双折射元件进行实现。

Result: 该几何框架成功建立了压缩态优化与几何相位之间的联系，揭示了压缩椭圆旋转由轨迹包围的立体角决定。框架具有普适性，适用于不同的物理平台。针对偏振压缩光系统，给出了基于连续变化双折射元件的可行实现方案。

Conclusion: 本文建立的几何框架为量子精密测量中压缩态的优化提供了系统性解决方案，揭示了态优化过程与几何相位的深刻联系。压缩椭圆的旋转本质上由路径几何决定，特别是轨迹包围的立体角。该框架具有广泛的适用性，可应用于多种物理平台，为实验实现测量最优压缩态提供了理论指导和实际途径。

Abstract: Preparation of optimal measurement states is a key requirement in quantum metrology utilizing squeezed states. We discuss a geometric framework that transforms an initially misaligned squeezed input into a measurement-optimal state in SU(2)-symmetric systems. Within this framework, the orientation of the squeezing ellipse constitutes an additional geometric degree of freedom and evolves as the mean state follows a controlled trajectory on the unit sphere. The resulting rotation of the ellipse is determined by the geometry of the path and, for the relevant class of transformations, depends on the solid angle enclosed by the trajectory, establishing a connection with the geometric phase. The discussed framework is applicable to different physical platforms. As a particular example, we consider polarization-squeezed light and outline a possible implementation using a continuously varying birefringent element.

</details>


### [118] [Lifting Lifted Product Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28621)
*Yuta Hirasaki,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群扩展和图提升的提升乘积(LP)码系统构造方法,通过保持Tanner图的局部结构来增加码字大小,并通过链映射和上链映射关联码参数、逻辑算子和容错逻辑操作模块,获得了参数更优的LP码,实现了低空间开销的码手术转移,并为代数定义的qLDPC码提出了热力学族的系统构造途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提升乘积码缺乏系统的构造方法来优化码参数和实现容错逻辑操作;需要在保持Tanner图局部结构的同时扩大码字规模;代数定义的qLDPC码缺乏没有底层欧几里得格子的热力学族系统定义方法;码手术模块在不同码族间的迁移存在空间开销问题。

Method: 基于群扩展和图提升构建LP码族的系统方法:通过有限提升增加码字大小同时保持Tanner图的局部结构;利用链映射和上链映射关联码族内的码参数、逻辑算子和容错逻辑操作模块;针对提升簇循环码开发并行乘积手术技术;通过选定的有限提升实现码手术模块在不同码间的转移;将提升作为系统化第一步来定义代数qLDPC码的热力学族,并通过相干信息的有限尺寸交叉进行分析。

Result: 获得了码参数优于先前报道的LP码;成功实现了码手术模块通过链映射在选定有限提升间的转移,在多种情况下降低了空间开销;为提升簇循环码开发了并行乘积手术方法;对多个基础码和选定提升,相干信息表现出有限尺寸交叉现象;提升方法为代数定义的qLDPC码提供了构造热力学族的系统途径。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于群扩展和图提升的LP码系统构造方法成功优化了码参数,降低了容错逻辑操作的空间开销,并为代数定义的qLDPC码建立热力学族提供了系统化框架。尽管相干信息显示出有限尺寸交叉的积极信号,但确定唯一码族仍需要额外的条件约束,这为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Lifted product (LP) codes form an important class of quantum error correcting codes with favorable code parameters. We introduce a systematic construction of LP code families based on group extensions and graph lifts. The construction increases the code size while preserving the local structure of the Tanner graph, and relates code parameters, logical operators, and fault-tolerant logical-operation gadgets within the families through chain and cochain maps.
  As a first application, we obtain LP codes with better code parameters than previously reported ones. We then demonstrate that code-surgery gadgets can be transferred across the selected finite lifts through chain maps and, in several cases, implemented with lower space overhead. We also develop parallel product surgery for lifted clustered cyclic codes. Finally, we propose lifting as a systematic first step toward defining thermodynamic families for algebraically defined qLDPC codes without an underlying Euclidean lattice. For several base codes and selected lifts, coherent information exhibits finite-size crossings, while our results also indicate that additional conditions are needed to determine a unique family.

</details>
